# Source-resolved Rust license texts for VTracer alpha.4

Generated from a **new** pinned Cargo resolution of the npm gitHead-matching source tag on 2026-11-11.
This is not evidence of the historical npm WASM build dependency graph.
Copies of original per-crate LICENSE/COPYING/NOTICE texts are included below to preserve individual attribution.
Local workspace crates use the upstream VTracer root MIT license. Any crate without a bundled
license file is covered by its declared SPDX expression and the generic license text in assets/licenses.
